     项且占比至少 70% 时，内容区顶部显示一次非模态提示`建议使用媒体预览`；应用不自动切换。这是待实测调优的初始阈值。\n78|\n79|- 左侧仍保留压缩包目录树。\n80|- 中央显示大尺寸安全预览。\n81|-
     底部显示当前目录的全部媒体项目条带，支持键盘、触控板、滚轮与多选；搜索结果跨目录时不显示条带，改用带`路径`列的扁平结果。\n82|- 右侧检查器显示类型、大小、压缩后大小、修改日期、路径等。\n83|-
     视频不自动带声音播放；使用系统播放控件。\n84|- 用户手动选定视图后，不再自动切换；选择按压缩包/目录在本地记忆，不写入压缩包。\n85|\n86|### 4.4 视图切换\n87|\n88|v1
     工具栏采用两个系统图标按钮切换列表与媒体预览；不显示尚未实现的图标/网格视图。控件有中文辅助功能标签：\n89|\n90|- `列表视图`\n91|- `媒体预览`\n92|\n93|### 4.5 Liquid Glass
     使用范围\n94|\n95|允许：标题栏、工具栏、侧边栏、检查器边缘、视图切换、紧凑进度/上下文控件。
     \n96|禁止：每个文件行套玻璃卡片、玻璃覆盖预览内容、装饰性重叠玻璃、影响文本对比度的自定义模糊。\n97|\n98|在“降低透明度”“增强对比度”“减少动态效果”开启时，使用系统回退表现，不手工模仿玻璃。若目标 SDK 的
     Liquid Glass API 不可用或行为变化，则退化为标准 macOS toolbar/sidebar/inspector
     系统材质；功能层级、间距、对比度和可访问性语义不得变化，不自行用自定义模糊复刻。\n99|\n100|## 5. 窗口与信息架构\n101|\n102|### 5.1
     无文档窗口\n103|\n104|首次打开或没有窗口时显示简洁欢迎页：\n105|\n106|- 主操作：`打开压缩包…`\n107|- 次操作：`创建归档…`\n108|- 最近使用列表。\n109|-
     可将文件/文件夹拖入窗口；拖入压缩包则打开，拖入普通项目则进入创建流程。\n110|- 不显示功能清单、营销卡片或空洞指标。\n111|\n112|默认文档窗口 1180 × 760 pt，最小 900 × 560 pt；低于能同
     时容纳三栏的宽度时，检查器先收起为工具栏入口，侧边栏再改为可呼出覆盖层，主文件列表不得被压到不可操作。\n113|\n114|顶部工具栏在默认宽度、最小宽度与放大窗口下不得发生按钮、标题、搜索框或系统交通灯重叠；固定阅读顺序为`压
     缩包标题 → 返回/前进 → 添加/解压缩 → 视图/信息 → 操作 → 搜索`。标题使用标题栏安全边距内的普通单行布局，不用可能越过窗口边缘的独立胶囊容器；默认宽度下两个主操作必须显示完整中文名称。`检测完整性`不是高频一级操作
     ，与`共享`、`标签`、`当前操作`等次要项一起进入有明确中文名称的`操作`菜单；仅在损坏、下载不完整或分卷异常等情形通过状态提示推荐检测。空间不足时再按`检查器收起 → 侧边栏覆盖`响应；不显示含义模糊的独立省略号`更多`按钮，
     也不得让系统把关键分组重排到搜索框之后。压缩包标题与条目文件名均保持单行；长名称采用中间省略以优先保留扩展名，完整名称通过悬停提示、辅助功能标签和信息栏提供，不允许自动换行破坏行高或缩略图条带节奏。媒体条带的圆角底和选中描边只包围
     缩略图；文件名与大小在其下方的透明背景上独立排版，不增加圆角文字容器，也不得与缩略图边框相交。\n115|\n116|### 5.2 已打开压缩包窗口\n117|\n118|- 标题：压缩包文件名。\n119|-
     副信息：格式、加密状态、项目数量、只读/可编辑状态。\n120|- 左侧使用单一归档侧边栏：`压缩包`目录树为主体，可折叠显示`收藏位置`；不得同时并排出现一套 Finder
     位置侧栏和第二套归档目录栏。操作记录不占用导航侧栏。主内容区始终只承载列表、搜索结果或媒体预览。\n121|- 中央：列表/媒体预览。\n122|- 右侧：`信息`/`预览`检查器。\n123|-
     底部状态：选择数量、总大小、完整性或暂存编辑状态。\n124|\n125|### 5.2.1 搜索结果\n126|\n127|搜索不改变压缩包层级数据。结果使用扁平列表，强制显示`路径`列，并保留类型、大小、修改日期和父目录跳转。
     清除搜索后恢复原目录、选择和滚动状态。搜索结果可预览、解压缩或定位到原位置；批量编辑前再次做全树冲突检查。\n128|\n129|### 5.2.2 常用位置\n130|\n131|应用本地维护`收藏位置`与最近使用的解压目标。创
     建/解压预设可以引用这些位置，但始终显示实际目标。移除收藏不删除磁盘内容。安全作用域书签失效时显示重新授权，不静默改用其他位置。\n132|\n133|### 5.3
     创建归档面板\n134|\n135|主要区域依次为：\n136|\n137|1. 待归档项目列表。\n138|2. `用途`预设：`发给 Windows
     用户`、`跨平台加密分享`、`兼容旧版解压软件`、`长期归档`、`自定义`。\n139|3. `格式`、`压缩方式`、`压缩级别`、`加密`、`分卷大小`。\n140|4. 文件名与 Windows 兼容预检结果。\n141|5.
     保存位置与文件名。\n142|6. 主按钮：`创建归档`。\n143|\n144|高级选项始终显示真实格式与接收方要求；预设不隐藏关键事实。\n145|\n146|### 5.4 解压缩面板\n147|\n148|-
     目标位置。\n149|- 范围：全部或所选项目。\n150|- 冲突策略：`逐项询问`、`保留两个文件`、`跳过`、`替换`；默认逐项询问；逐项面板提供`对其余冲突应用此决定`。\n151|-
     安全摘要：预计项目数/大小、异常路径、符号链接、资源风险。\n152|- `解压缩后在 Finder 中显示`开关；仅定位并选中结果，不直接打开任何文件。\n153|-
     主按钮：`解压缩`。\n154|\n155|常用/最近目标以菜单形式提供，不挤占主面板。任何预设都不能包含解压后自动运行脚本或程序。\n156|\n157|### 5.5 设置窗口\n158|\n159|原生 macOS
     `Settings` 场景，v1 使用六个一级分类并定义默认值：\n160|\n161|- `通用`：启动时显示欢迎页（开）；恢复上次未关闭窗口（开）；最近使用数量（10）。\n162|-
     `解压缩`：默认目标（每次询问）；冲突策略（逐项询问）；完成后在 Finder 中显示（关）；保留 quarantine（开）。\n163|- `创建归档`：默认用途（发给 Windows 用户）；默认排除 macOS
     元数据（开且适用于所有创建格式）；保存前自动检测完整性（开）。\n164|- `兼容性`：旧文件名编码（自动检测）；可按当前压缩包临时选择代码页；Windows 路径预检（开）。自动检测顺序为格式明确标志/BOM、严格
     UTF-8、受支持代码页启发式；置信度不足时必须询问，不静默猜测。\n165|- `密码与外部工具`：钥匙串保存策略（默认不保存）；管理已保存密码；选择并验证 RARLAB `rar`；显示 7zz 版本与许可证。\n166|-
     `隐私与高级`：诊断路径脱敏（始终开）；预览缓存上限（默认 1 GiB）；并发外部任务（默认 2、范围 1–2）；重置视图状态。安全硬限制不可在此关闭。\n167|\n168|## 6.
     工具栏、菜单与快捷键\n169|\n170|### 6.1 默认工具栏\n171|\n172|- `打开` — `⌘O`\n173|- `创建归档` — `⇧⌘N`\n174|- `添加` —
     `⌥⌘A`（可编辑压缩包）\n175|- `移除` — `⌫`（有选择且可编辑）\n176|- `重命名` — `Return`\n177|- `解压缩` — `⌘E`\n178|- 视图切换\n179|- `信息` —
     `⌥⌘I`\n180|- `操作`菜单 — 检测完整性、共享、标签、当前操作等低频项\n181|- 搜索 — `⌘F`\n182|\n183|`保存`使用 `⌘S`；`另存为…`使用 `⇧⌘S`。Space
     打开/关闭预览；`⌘1`为列表视图，`⌘2`为媒体预览，`⌘,`打开设置，`⌘?`打开帮助。Return 第一次进入重命名编辑，第二次确认，Escape
     取消。所有快捷键需检查与系统菜单冲突，并在实现阶段用真实菜单验证。\n184|\n185|有次要选项的工具栏操作使用清晰的相邻菜单箭头或独立菜单，不依赖用户发现“长按按钮”。\n186|\n187|工具栏响应式验收至少覆盖 900
     pt、1180 pt 与最大化宽度；自动化检查可见控件框不相交，长中文、拉丁字母、组合字符与长扩展名夹具不换行，且完整文件名仍可由 VoiceOver/帮助文本取得。\n188|\n189|### 6.2
     主菜单\n190|\n191|- `文件`：打开、最近使用、关闭、创建归档、保存、另存为、解压缩、完整性检测。v1 不显示尚未完整设计和实现的转换入口。\n192|-
     `编辑`：撤销/重做、剪切/复制名称、全选、添加、移除、重命名、替换。\n193|- `显示`：列表/媒体预览、显示/隐藏侧边栏、检查器、预览、列设置、排序。\n194|-
     `归档`：添加、移除、重命名、加密设置、分卷、检测完整性、修复（可用时）。\n195|- `窗口`与`帮助`遵循系统默认结构。\n196|\n197|## 7. 中文术语基线\n198|\n199|| 概念 | 界面用语 |
     使用说明 |\n200||---|---|---|\n201|| archive noun | `压缩包`（面向用户）/`归档`（系统命令和格式语境） | 避免机械统一；上下文一致 |\n202|| create archive
     | `创建归档` | 对齐当前 macOS |\n203|| extract | `解压缩` | 主命令；不用`展开`或孤立的`提取` |\n204|| open | `打开` | 打开压缩包或预览 |\n205|| add
     entry | `添加` | 添加到当前压缩包 |\n206|| remove entry | `移除` | 未保存前可撤销；不用`删除文件`误导源文件 |\n207|| rename entry | `重命名` | 暂存更改
     |\n208|| replace entry | `替换内容` | 明确不是同名覆盖整个压缩包 |\n209|| save | `保存更改`/菜单`保存` | 7z 等需显示重新生成代价 |\n210|| save as |
     `另存为…` | 分卷编辑或只读位置 |\n211|| test | `检测完整性` | 不使用模糊的`测试压缩包` |\n212|| repair | `尝试修复副本…` | 永不覆盖唯一源文件 |\n213|| split
     archive | `分卷压缩包`/`分卷大小` | 缺卷用`缺少分卷文件` |\n214|| encrypted | `已加密` | 与“需要密码”“不支持的加密方式”分开 |\n215|| settings | `设置…` |
     不用旧称`偏好设置` |\n216|| keychain | `钥匙串` | 对齐 macOS |\n217|| queue/history | `当前操作`/`近期操作`/`操作历史` |
     按时间尺度区分，不把运行任务与历史混在一起 |\n218|| Windows native | `Windows 11 可直接打开` | 不显示 W0 |\n219|| third-party required | `接收方需要
     7-Zip 或 WinRAR` | 不显示 W1 |\n220|\n221|最终字符串必须进入 String Catalog，带使用场景、语法和辅助功能注释；不能散落硬编码。\n222|\n223|## 8.
     格式与能力注册表\n224|\n225|`ArchiveCapabilityRegistry` 是唯一真相来源。能力键至少包含：\n226|\n227|- 格式与实际探测结果。\n228|-
     操作：列出、读取、预览、创建、更新、加密、分卷、检测、修复。\n229|- 加密/压缩方法。\n230|- 主引擎、只读诊断回退。\n231|- 是否需要完整重建。\n232|- 是否需要外部工具及其验证状态。\n233|-
     当前构建配置可用性。\n234|- 用户可读的不可用原因。\n235|\n236|确定性路由：\n237|\n238|- ZIP：minizip-ng。\n239|- 7z：`7zz`。\n240|- TAR/CPIO 与
     GZIP/BZIP2/XZ/Zstandard：libarchive。\n241|- RAR 读取：`7zz`，libarchive 仅作支持范围内的只读诊断。\n242|- RAR 创建：外部 RARLAB
     `rar`。\n243|- ISO：libarchive。\n244|- DMG：macOS
     磁盘映像流程。\n245|\n246|任何编辑事务开始后不得切换引擎。回退只能在打开/诊断阶段发生，并向用户说明原因。\n247|\n248|ZIP 中出现 minizip-ng 不支持的条目级压缩/加密方法时，仍由
     minizip-ng 保持文档所有权：可安全列举的其他条目继续浏览，该条目标为`压缩方式暂不支持`并禁用预览/解压/替换；不得把同一编辑事务暗中切换给 `7zz`。用户主动执行`用诊断引擎检查`时，`7zz`
     只返回独立诊断，不改变文档能力快照或保存引擎。RAR 的 libarchive 回退同样只用于诊断；即使诊断引擎声称可读，也不自动用它发布解压结果。\n249|\n250|## 9. 领域模型\n251|\n252|### 9.1
     ArchiveDocument\n253|\n254|- 源 URL/安全作用域书签。\n255|- 格式探测与能力快照。\n256|- 源文件身份、元数据和 SHA-256。\n257|- 条目索引。\n258|-
     当前目录/选择/视图状态。\n259|- 暂存更改集。\n260|- 活动操作与恢复状态。\n261|\n262|### 9.2 ArchiveEntry\n263|\n264|- 稳定会话内 ID。\n265|-
     原始路径字节与解码显示名分离。\n266|- 规范化比较键，仅用于冲突检测。\n267|- 类型、压缩/原始大小、CRC/摘要（若有）、时间、权限、链接信息、加密状态。\n268|-
     预览支持与安全分类。\n269|\n270|### 9.3 PendingChange\n271|\n272|-
     `add`、`remove`、`rename`、`replace`、`metadataPolicyChange`。\n273|- 原条目 ID、来源 URL、目标路径。\n274|- 冲突/编码/兼容性检查结果。\n275|-
     用户可撤销顺序。\n276|\n277|### 9.4 ArchiveOperation\n278|\n279|- 操作 ID、类型、阶段、进度、可取消性。\n280|- 引擎/提供方与版本。\n281|-
     输入/输出、结构化警告与错误。\n282|- 已脱敏的诊断信息。\n283|\n284|操作对象可序列化到本地操作记录，但不能转换成可执行 shell
     字符串。调度器支持顺序任务、每压缩包修改互斥、最多两个外部任务及系统资源感知；操作记录保存引擎/版本/提供方身份以便复现。\n285|\n286|## 10. 打开、索引和浏览流程\n287|\n288|1.
     用户选择或拖入文件。\n289|2. 读取签名并探测格式；扩展名仅作提示。\n290|3. 能力注册表选择唯一主读取器。\n291|4.
     需要密码时显示安全输入，不把“密码错误”“没有提供密码”“不支持加密”混为一谈。\n292|5. 增量读取目录并先显示可用内容；后台完成索引、摘要和安全扫描。\n293|6. 格式探测、索引和 SHA-256
     使用同一个已打开文件描述符；保存打开快照。哈希完成前可以只读浏览，但不得开始保存事务。\n294|7. 恢复该压缩包本地视图状态。\n295|8. 若当前目录满足媒体建议规则且用户从未手动选择视图，可建议媒体预览；不强制跳变。\n
     296|\n297|启动时收到压缩包文件参数时直接将其作为初始文档打开。旧编码压缩包的原始路径字节始终保留；用户覆盖编码只改变显示/导出解释，不改源。编辑旧编码 ZIP 后保存时，未改变的原条目保留原始名称字段；新增或改名条目写入
     UTF-8 并设置 bit 11。若结果产生混合编码，保存前明确提示并允许改为`全部名称转换为
     UTF-8`后另存为，绝不静默重编码。\n298|\n299|打开失败分类：格式不支持、文件损坏、目录损坏、密码缺失、密码错误、加密不支持、缺少分卷、提供方不可用、权限不足、资源限制。\n300|\n301|## 11.
     预览流程\n302|\n303|1. 选择条目后先判断类型、大小、加密与安全分类。\n304|2. 每个窗口创建随机 `0700` 缓存根目录。\n305|3. 条目 ID
     映射为随机磁盘名，仅保留验证后的显示扩展名；压缩包路径绝不直接成为缓存路径。\n306|4. 使用排他/不跟随链接方式创建并执行规范化包含检查。\n307|5. 受每文件 256 MiB、每窗口 1 GiB、30
     秒约束；单条目预计解压比例超过 50:1 时不生成预览，显示`此项目的压缩比例异常，请解压缩后查看`。该预览比例限制不可覆盖；如无法可靠获得压缩后/展开大小，也不在预览路径中试探性展开。\n308|6. 添加
     quarantine；特殊文件和链接不进入预览。\n309|7. 预览是明确的格式能力，不以“系统也许能打开”代替产品承诺：图片使用原生图像视图；PDF 使用
     PDFKit；Word（`.doc`/`.docx`）、Excel（`.xls`/`.xlsx`）和 PowerPoint（`.ppt`/`.pptx`）使用系统 Quick Look
     只读预览；视频使用原生播放器且不自动带声音播放；纯文本使用受限文本查看器。Office/Quick Look 输入必须是通过本节安全流程产生的随机缓存文件，不得把压缩包路径或未校验输出直接交给预览器。\n310|8. Quick
     Look 对受支持 Office 文档返回失败、超时或空预览时，界面显示明确的`无法生成预览`及`解压缩后打开`操作，不伪装为成功；这不降低“正常有效的 Word/Excel/PPT 必须可预览”的验收要求。实现必须使用真实
     `.doc/.docx/.xls/.xlsx/.ppt/.pptx` 夹具逐类验证，并记录 macOS/Quick Look 版本。\n311|9.
     窗口关闭时删除缓存；启动时清理过期缓存。\n312|\n313|所有预览缓存位于固定应用缓存根下的随机窗口 UUID 子目录。启动时在没有任何窗口活跃的阶段删除所有遗留窗口子目录，不依赖条目映射表或压缩包路径判断“过期”；清理失败
     记录诊断并在下一次启动重试。\n314|\n315|内嵌压缩包不会自动递归。用户双击或从上下文菜单选择`在归档工作台中打开`时，才创建隔离子会话。整个会话链共享最多 3 层、10,000 项、1 GiB 展开字节和 60
     秒预算；任一预算耗尽即停止，不允许覆盖。\n316|\n317|## 12. 解压缩流程与冲突处理\n318|\n319|1. 先建立完整目标计划，不边发现边无提示写入。\n320|2.
     拒绝绝对路径、`..`、空组件、NUL、越界路径和目标根外的规范化结果。\n321|3. 符号链接、硬链接与特殊文件按安全策略分类；默认不允许链接逃逸。\n322|4.
     估计条目数、展开大小、压缩比、路径深度和可用空间。\n323|5. 超过默认安全预算时阻止并解释；路径结构、链接逃逸和无法可靠列举的输入不可覆盖。预计总体或任一条目解压比例超过 100:1
     时先阻止，用户仅可在重新确认目标空间、展开大小和风险后对显式解压缩单次覆盖；预览不可覆盖。\n324|6. 名称冲突表一次性列出，也支持逐项决定；不能静默覆盖。\n325|7.
     以临时名称写入、验证后发布；部分失败有清晰结果清单。\n326|8. 可选显示目标目录；不自动运行任何内容。\n327|\n328|默认阻断阈值假设：预计输出超过 50 GiB 与目标可用空间 80% 中较小者、超过
     1,000,000 项、路径深度超过 128、总体或任一条目解压比例超过 100:1。实施基准可以收紧非结构性阈值，不得在无安全评审时放宽。\n329|\n330|### 12.1
     外部引擎安全契约\n331|\n332|`7zz` 的人类可读 stdout/stderr 只用于诊断，绝不作为路径安全、成功或完整性的唯一数据源。能由 minizip-ng/libarchive 的结构化 API
     安全列举的格式先用结构化元数据建立目标计划。必须由 `7zz` 处理的加密/特殊 7z 或 RAR 始终先解到仅该任务可访问的随机暂存根；helper 不获得最终目标写权限。应用随后以文件描述符为根、不跟随链接地枚举实际产物，逐项
     执行包含性、类型、条目数、字节数和比例检查，全部通过后才发布到用户目标。任何无法结构化预检、越界产物、控制字符、未知节点类型、输出截断、版本协议不匹配或预算超限均整单失败并清理暂存，不尝试从文本行“修复”路径。\n333|\n33
     4|## 13. 创建归档流程\n335|\n336|### 13.1 Windows 原生预设\n337|\n338|`发给 Windows 用户`：\n339|\n340|- 未加密 ZIP。\n341|-
     Store/Deflate。\n342|- UTF-8，local/central header 均设置 bit 11，无 BOM，不额外写冗余 Unicode Path 字段。\n343|- 默认排除
     `.DS_Store`、`._*`、`__MACOSX` 和非必要 macOS 元数据。\n344|- 对 NFC、大小写、Windows 保留名、非法字符、尾随空格/点和路径预算做全树预检。\n345|\n346|###
     13.2 加密预设\n347|\n348|- `跨平台加密分享`：7z AES-256 且加密文件名，显示`接收方需要 7-Zip 或 WinRAR`。\n349|- `兼容旧版解压软件`：ZipCrypto
     ZIP，显示`兼容性较高，但密码保护较弱`。\n350|- AES ZIP 显示第三方工具要求，不声称 Windows Explorer 可直接打开。\n351|- 任何创建格式默认排除
     `.DS_Store`、`._*`、`__MACOSX`；用户可在自定义选项中显式保留，但 Windows 预设不可保留。\n352|- `发给 Windows 用户`与分卷同时启用时撤销`Windows 11
     可直接打开`标记，并显示`Windows 资源管理器无法直接打开分卷压缩包；接收方需要 7-Zip 或 WinRAR`。\n353|\n354|### 13.3
     文件名冲突表\n355|\n356|列：`原名称`、`输出名称`、`原因`、`接收方影响`。操作：逐项改名、应用可预览的批量规则、移除项目、切换保留原名的专业模式。每次改变后重新做全树冲突检测。\n357|\n358|###
     13.4 创建完成\n359|\n360|创建成功不只看进程退出码：重新打开、检测完整性、核对条目清单；失败保留诊断并清理/记录暂存文件。\n361|\n362|## 14. 编辑与保存事务\n363|\n364|### 14.1
     暂存编辑\n365|\n366|添加、移除、重命名和替换首先进入本地 `PendingChange`，底部显示`N 项更改尚未保存`。用户可以逐项或全部撤销。\n367|\n368|### 14.2
     单文件压缩包保存\n369|\n370|1. 检查目标目录可写和空间（空间仅作预估）。\n371|2. 在目标同目录创建 `.ArchiveApp.<UUID>.staging`。\n372|3.
     使用固定主引擎生成完整结果。\n373|4. 核对条目清单、摘要和引擎完整性结果。\n374|5. fsync 暂存文件和父目录。\n375|6. 通过 `NSFileCoordinator`
     将源复核和替换纳入同一个协调写入块；块内重新检查设备/文件身份/大小/纳秒时间戳和 SHA-256。\n376|7. 若源已变化，中止并提示`原压缩包已被其他应用更改`。\n377|8.
     在同一协调块内原子替换，释放协调后再次验证。\n378|\n379|### 14.3 只读位置\n380|\n381|源目录不可写时禁用原地`保存`，提供`另存为…`，并在用户选择的新目标旁暂存。\n382|\n383|###
     14.4 分卷压缩包\n384|\n385|文件集不能原子替换。创建时在同级事务目录生成/验证完整分卷后，以新基名发布。编辑已有分卷始终使用`另存为…`，不提供虚假原地保存。\n386|\n387|### 14.5
     重建成本\n388|\n389|7z 或其他需完整重建的操作在执行前显示预计读取/写入量与`保存时将重新生成此压缩包`，提供可取消进度；solid 7z 明确提示可能需要读取和重写整个 solid 块。取消、provider
     失败、校验失败或崩溃后的重建暂存一律视为不可发布；可安全识别时自动删除，删除失败则进入恢复记录供用户显示/清理，绝不改变源或把部分结果改名为正式压缩包。\n390|\n391|### 14.6
     外部应用编辑回写\n392|\n393|用户选择`用外部应用编辑副本`时：\n394|\n395|1. 把条目提取到本窗口独立、受控、带 quarantine 的编辑目录。\n396|2. 记录初始文件身份和
     SHA-256。\n397|3. 由系统打开副本；应用监视已保存变化，不监视任意外部目录。\n398|4. 检测到变化后显示原/新大小、修改时间和可预览比较，询问`替换压缩包中的内容`或`忽略更改`。\n399|5.
     用户接受时重新读取并把当前内容快照复制到事务暂存区，计算 SHA-256 后与 `replace` 更改绑定；之后外部编辑器再次保存会产生新的变化提示，不会悄悄改变已暂存版本。仍需用户保存压缩包。\n400|6.
     关闭窗口时未处理变化必须提示；不自动回写。\n401|\n402|### 14.7 修复副本\n403|\n404|v1 的`尝试修复副本…`只对 ZIP
     出现，将可恢复条目写入新文件，例如`原名称（已修复）.zip`，显示“无法保证恢复全部内容”，并在完成后重新检测。RAR/7z
     等格式在能力、许可证和真实损坏夹具未独立验证前不显示修复按钮。修复永不覆盖源。\n405|\n406|## 15. 崩溃恢复\n407|\n408|-
     恢复日志存于应用支持目录，只记录安全作用域书签、源指纹、暂存身份、操作和阶段，不记录密码；每条记录使用钥匙串内随机本机密钥做 HMAC，验证失败即隔离并记录诊断，绝不恢复或提交。\n409|-
     启动时扫描未完成事务；永不自动提交。\n410|- 源和暂存均重新验证后提供`继续恢复`、`显示临时文件`、`删除临时文件`。\n411|- 源已变化或暂存损坏时只允许保留/删除，不允许覆盖源。\n412|-
     磁盘不足、强制终止和每个事务阶段均需故障注入测试。\n413|- 已完成/废弃恢复记录保留 30 天或合计 50 MiB（先到者清理）；正在恢复的事务不按时限自动删除。\n414|\n415|## 16.
     外部提供方与进程模型\n416|\n417|v1 每项操作启动一个短生命周期进程，不使用共享常驻池。\n418|\n419|- 每项任务独立 `0700` 工作目录。\n420|-
     全局最多两个外部提供方任务，同一压缩包仅一个修改任务。\n421|- 不使用 shell；固定可执行文件；显式 argv；选项终止保护；最小环境。\n422|- stdout/stderr
     并发读取到有界环形缓冲；溢出终止任务，不以截断输出判断成功。\n423|- 取消先温和终止，超时后强制终止；所有输出仅在暂存位置。\n424|- 版本范围、架构、身份和输出协议均需匹配。\n425|- 调度默认
     FIFO；用户可以取消排队任务和把尚未开始的任务移到队首。v1 不提供暂停/恢复正在运行的编解码，只提供取消；崩溃恢复属于独立事务恢复语义。\n426|- 外部工具默认使用 quiet/机器可控模式；stdout/stderr
     环形缓冲只收集异常诊断，不承载条目清单或安全关键进度。正常进度来自 provider 的结构化回调、受控进度通道或文件系统计量；意外输出超限仍终止任务。\n427|- libarchive 写盘不在主进程临时修改全局
     `umask`。每个 libarchive 写入任务在独立短生命周期 helper 中以固定 `077` umask 启动，再由显式权限策略发布；因此不会与其他应用任务争用进程级 umask。\n428|-
     并发锁分为文档读快照与保存排他锁：浏览/搜索/预览/解压可共享同一已打开快照；保存获取排他锁，期间新的只读任务提示`正在保存，请稍后`，既有只读任务先完成或取消后才提交替换。\n429|\n430|### 16.1
     RARLAB\n431|\n432|用户在`设置 > 外部工具`中明确选择或确认 `rar`：\n433|\n434|1. 解析符号链接。\n435|2. 验证常规文件、Apple
     Silicon、所有者为用户/root，且组/其他用户不可写。\n436|3. 在执行前使用 Security.framework 静态代码 API 验证签名有效性、非 ad-hoc，并将签名链/Team ID 与项目维护的已验证
     RARLAB 发布者清单匹配；若官方当前二进制不满足该条件，v1 RAR 创建保持禁用，不以版本字符串替代身份验证。\n437|4. 显示许可证/外部依赖说明、签名身份和二进制 SHA-256，由用户确认。\n438|5.
     在只授权随机探测目录、无用户文件权限的隔离进程中执行创建、检测、解压缩探测并核对哈希；该步骤不证明后续输入可信，真实任务仍受通用进程模型和暂存发布限制。\n439|6. 路径、文件身份、签名、版本或 SHA-256
     变化时自动禁用，要求重新验证。\n440|\n441|RAR 创建的通过条件必须全部满足：`rar` 返回成功；`rar t` 完整性检测无错误；用 RARLAB 自身解压到隔离目录后条目清单、大小和 SHA-256
     与输入清单一致；再由固定版本 `7zz` 独立列举并解压比对一致。任何一步失败都不发布输出。Windows WinRAR 物理验收是 Direct/OpenSource
     发布门槛，不是本机单次创建的静默替代项。\n442|\n443|RARLAB 二进制文件不复制到项目、应用包、网站包或开源仓库。\n444|\n445|## 17. DMG 与
     ISO\n446|\n447|DMG：只读、no-auto-open、no-browse 方式挂载，读取机器可解析结果并记录挂载身份；不运行内容；窗口关闭时卸载；失败可重试；启动时恢复应用遗留挂载。原始提取只有在夹具矩阵证明
     UDZO/UDBZ/UDRW、加密与损坏 UDIF、sparse/sparsebundle、资源叉、符号链接、超大文件、取消和校验失败都不会越权或损坏数据后才启用；未通过时只提供只读挂载浏览。若 App
     Sandbox/hardened runtime spike 证明当前配置不能可靠挂载，则能力注册表把 DMG 标为`此构建仅支持识别，无法浏览磁盘映像`，不尝试放宽全局权限或伪装成可用。
     \n448|ISO：libarchive 只读浏览/提取，覆盖 ISO9660、Joliet、Rock Ridge、UDF 夹具。\n449|\n450|## 18. 密码与钥匙串\n451|\n452|-
     密码使用安全输入框，不出现在日志、命令显示、错误详情或分析信息中。\n453|- 默认只在当前操作内存中使用。\n454|- 用户可选择`将密码储存在钥匙串中`。钥匙串密码项使用随机 UUID
     主键；本地元数据只保存用户标签、安全作用域书签、卷/文件资源标识、创建时间和内容指纹，不保存密码。\n455|-
     打开时先解析书签并匹配文件资源标识；同卷重命名/移动后更新书签。精确内容指纹只用于识别未改变的复制件；编辑保存成功后在同一密码项上追加新指纹并淘汰旧的“当前位置”关联。\n456|-
     资源标识与指纹都不匹配时不自动尝试任何候选密码，提示重新输入并允许用户明确关联；网络卷、备份恢复和复制场景必须经过该保守分支。\n457|- 错误状态区分：没有提供、密码错误、不支持的加密方式、文件损坏。\n458|-
     ZipCrypto 的弱安全性在创建前明确提示。\n459|- 密码以可控的字节缓冲传递，避免长期 Swift `String` 副本；C 适配器只在最短作用域创建本地缓冲，使用后显式清零。对 7zz/RARLAB，固定版本
     provider 仅可使用工具自身的交互式密码模式：启动参数只含不带密码值的提示开关，helper 通过该子进程独占、不可继承的 stdin/伪终端写入一次密码字节并立即清零；禁止 argv、环境变量、普通/临时文件、剪贴板和
     shell。若经 Xcode spike 证明某个固定版本无法在该合同下可靠工作，则该版本的加密操作保持禁用，不退回泄漏机制。不承诺对已被系统/框架复制的内存绝对擦除，但
     crash/诊断收集必须排除密码字段。\n460|\n461|## 19. Windows 与多语言互操作\n462|\n463|内部合同：\n464|\n465|- W0：Windows 11 Explorer 原生，未加密
     Store/Deflate ZIP。\n466|- W1：当前 7-Zip/WinRAR 可用的加密 ZIP/7z、分卷和 RAR。\n467|- W2：TAR
     系列/专业工具或命令行场景。\n468|\n469|普通界面不用内部代码，只显示接收要求。\n470|\n471|Windows 文件名规则基于 Microsoft 官方规则；每组件最多 255 UTF-16 code
     units。W0 使用 180 UTF-16 code units 的保守压缩包相对路径预算，并明确这是为常见提取目录预留空间，不等于所有环境保证。\n472|\n473|物理验收必须覆盖 Windows 11 24H2
     Explorer、当前 Windows 7-Zip、当前 WinRAR；对多语言名称、ZIP64、4 GiB 边界、分卷、加密、Mac 元数据清理和解压后哈希逐项证明。\n474|\n475|## 20. Finder
     与系统集成\n476|\n477|- 文档类型关联和`打开方式`。\n478|- Finder 快速操作：`创建归档`、`解压缩到此处`（安全选项与冲突时打开主应用）。\n479|- Quick Look Preview
     Extension 只读、资源受限；不使用旧 `.qlgenerator`。\n480|- Finder Sync 不用于普通压缩包菜单。\n481|- Services/App Intents/Shortcuts
     只暴露可安全、可解释的操作。\n482|- 文档类型包含常见首卷/后续卷模式；分卷发现按格式规则和同目录授权进行，不依赖只注册主扩展名。\n483|\n484|分卷发现先按 ZIP `.zNN`/`.zip`、7z
     `.7z.NNN`、RAR `.partNN.rar`/旧式 `.rNN` 的明确规则生成候选，再为每卷记录文件资源标识、大小、修改时间和 SHA-256
     快照。操作开始前全部复核；发现同名身份变化、编号缺口、重复卷或越出已授权目录即停止并要求用户确认/重新选择，不把目录中“名字看起来像”的文件直接加入集合。\n485|\n486|## 21.
     操作记录与诊断\n487|\n488|操作信息分三层：工具栏`当前操作`弹出层显示正在运行/排队任务；同一弹出层底部显示最近 10 项完成结果；菜单`窗口 > 操作历史`打开独立窗口并按保留策略显示更早记录。操作历史不作为默认导航
     侧栏。类型、文件显示名、时间、状态和可恢复操作一致，默认不永久保存完整路径。\n489|\n490|错误详情包含：稳定错误代码、用户可读原因、建议操作、格式/引擎/版本、已脱敏路径和可复制诊断摘要。不得包含密码、密钥、完整私人路径
     或压缩包内容。\n491|\n492|压缩包级注释和格式属性只在 provider 能无损、结构化读取时显示在`信息`检查器；v1
     默认只读，不因一次普通内容编辑而丢弃。若目标格式保存会丢失它们，保存前列入元数据损失清单并要求另存为确认。\n493|\n494|## 22. 错误分类与用户动作\n495|\n496|| 分类 | 示例用语 | 主要动作

     verification evidence or state the remaining risk plainly.\n502|\n503|### Reviewer View\n504|\n505|- What bug,
     logic gap, missing test, unsupported claim, or overcomplication would a reviewer flag?\n506|- What evidence proves
     the likely failure points have been handled?\n507|- Does every changed line or paragraph serve the task?\n508|- Are

     concise enough to act on?\n519|\n520|### Third-Party View\n521|\n522|- If someone unfamiliar with the conversation

`ArchiveDomain`：文档、条目、更改、能力、错误和操作模型。\n571|- `ArchiveCore`：用例协调、索引、搜索、策略。\n572|- `ArchiveTransactions`：暂存、验证、提交、恢复。\n573|- `ArchiveSecurity`：路径、链接、资源预算、预览隔离、红action。\n574|-  the Windows compatibility benefit. I think "创建可由 Windows 11 直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包" reads

2. **"创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP 压缩包"** (line 352, header subtitle) — This says "send

Wait, actually I need to re-read more carefully. Line 352: `"创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP

 ZIP archives that Windows users can directly open. The "直接" should modify the Windows

 "send directly without intermediate steps" or "Windows users can directly open".
 

Natural reading: "创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP 压缩包" = "Create ZIP archives that can be

 for sending (like email attachment ready), but the real point is Windows compatibility

Better: `创建 Windows 用户可直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包` — this puts "directly" next to "open" where

Now let me also look for consistency issues:

**Consistency / Overclaim Audit:**

1. The design spec uses "Windows 11" specifically. The strings use "Windows 11" and
 "Windows" interchangeably:
   - Line 98: "Windows 11"
   - Line 233: "Windows" (generic)
   - Line 352: "Windows" (generic, in "Windows 用户")
   - Line 414: "Windows" (generic)
   - Line 554: "Windows" (generic)
   - Line 556: "Windows" (generic)

Is this inconsistent? The design spec (section 19) specifies physical verification
 on "Windows 11 24H2 Explorer". But the error messages and compatibility checks apply
 to Windows in general (reserved names, illegal characters are consistent across Windows
 versions). The compatibility claim specifically says "Windows 11 可直接打开" because that
's the verified target. Using "Windows" in error/check contexts is actually more accurate
 because the filename rules apply to Windows broadly, not just Windows 11. The specific
 claim "Windows 11 可直接打开" is appropriately scoped.

So this isn't truly inconsistent — it's correct:
- Compatibility CLAIM: "Windows 11" (verified target)
- Error/check context: "Windows" (rules apply broadly)

2. The tooltip on line 98 says "Windows 11" while the subtitle on line 352 says "
Windows 用户". The tooltip is on the welcome screen button, the subtitle is in the creation
 panel. Both could use "Windows 11" for consistency, but "Windows 用户" (Windows users
) is more natural in the context of "send to Windows users". I'll flag this as optional
 polish.

3. "归档" vs "压缩包" consistency: The design spec (section 7) says 压缩包 for user-facing
, 归档 for system/command context. Let me check:
   - Line 97: `"创建归档"` — This is a user-facing button. But "创建归档" is the design spec
's approved term for "create archive". ✅
   - Line 140: `"无法创建压缩包"` — User-facing alert. Uses 压缩包. Consistent with design spec
? The spec says 压缩包 for user-facing. Alert is user-facing, uses 压缩包. But "创建归档" (
button) vs "创建压缩包" (alert) — is this inconsistency?

Actually, the design spec section 7 says:
- archive noun: `压缩包` (user-facing) / `归档` (system/format context)
- create archive: `创建归档`

So the button says "创建归档" (the action), and the error says "无法创建压缩包" (the thing that
 couldn't be created). This is actually consistent with the design spec: the action
 is "创建归档", but the thing being created (the resulting file) is described as "压缩包
" when mentioned in isolation.

Now wait — the footer note on line 469 says "创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件" — this uses neither
 归档 nor 压缩包. But that's fine because it's referring to "全部文件" (all files), not to
 the archive itself.

4. Overclaim about Windows compatibility:
   - Line 98 tooltip: "创建可由 Windows 11 直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包" — This appears unconditionally
 on the welcome screen button, even though the actual compatibility depends on the
 preset and pre-check results. Is this an overclaim? The button opens a creation panel
 where the preset defaults to "发给 Windows 用户" which produces Windows-compatible ZIP
s. But the user could theoretically switch to a different preset (e.g., encrypted
 7z) that wouldn't be Windows 11 native. The tooltip is associated with the button
, not the final output, so it describes the intended/default behavior. This is arguably
 acceptable for a tooltip — it describes what the feature aims to do. I'll flag it
 as optional polish rather than must-change.

Actually, looking more carefully: the design spec section 5.1 shows the welcome screen
 has "创建归档…" as a secondary action, and section 5.3 shows the creation panel. The
 default preset is "发给 Windows 用户". The tooltip describes the default/intended behavior
. It's fine.

I notice I should also consider whether the string "创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件。" (line 469

 creation as complete" rather than "before the creation process finishes." In that

 step happens as part of the creation process, before completion is declared.

 with or as part of the creation, which is misleading if the verification happens
 after the archive is written but before we tell the user it's done.

Actually, looking at the code flow:
1. `createWindowsZIP` creates the ZIP file
2. After creation succeeds (line 295): `apply(snapshot)` — this opens and applies
 the snapshot
3. Then `creationProgress = 1`, `lastCreatedURL = outputURL`, status/operation messages
 set

So the flow is: write ZIP → open and verify → mark complete. The "创建完成前" could mean
 "before we tell you it's complete, we verify." If the footer note is telling users
 what will happen after they click the button, this timing is actually accurate! The
 verification is the last step before completion is reported.

However, the phrasing is still ambiguous. "创建完成前" in common Chinese usage means "
before [the act of] creation finishes" not "before [we report] creation as complete
." And from a user perspective, they see a progress bar fill up during creation, and
 the verification might happen after the progress reaches 100% but before the success
 message — the user would see this as "after creation."

I'll keep this as must-change. A clearer phrase would be: `创建完成后将重新打开并核对全部文件。`

4. **"选择保存位置和文件名；已有文件不会被静默覆盖。"** (line 246) — Save panel message. The phrase "静默覆盖" (silently overwrite) is clear. "已有文件" (existing files) is clear. This is good.

5. **"创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件。"** (line 469) — Footer note before creation. "创建完成前" means "before creation completes". This is awkward because the action of "creating" hasn't started yet — the user is about to click "创建归档". The message should set expectations about what will happen after clicking the button.

The intended meaning (from design spec section 13.4): after creation, the app will reopen and verify the archive. So the message should say something like: `创建后将重新打开并核对全部文件` (will reopen after creation) rather than "before creation completes" which sounds like it happens during creation.

This is a timing/action confusion. The user hasn't clicked create yet, and the message says "before creation completes, it will reopen and verify". But the reopening happens AFTER creation, not before it completes. The message is misleading about when this verification occurs.

Suggested: `创建完成后将重新打开并核对全部文件。` or `将在创建完成后重新打开并核对全部文件。`

6. **"发给 Windows 用户"** (line 40) — Purpose string. Matches design spec section 13.1 exactly: `发给 Windows 用户`. Good.

7. **"标准压缩"** (line 42) — Compression method. "标准" is somewhat vague. What does "standard compression" mean in the context of ZIP? It means Deflate (as per section 13.1: "Store/Deflate"). For a user-facing string, "标准压缩" is acceptable but could be more precise if needed. However, since the design spec doesn't mandate a specific term here, I'll note it as optional polish.

8. **"Windows 11 可直接打开"** (line 44) — Compatibility string. This matches the design spec section 7 exactly: `Windows 11 可直接打开`. Good.

9. **"待归档项目 · \(draft.inputs.count) 项"** (line 360) — GroupBox label. "待归档项目" = "Items to be archived". Fine. The separator " · " is fine. The count format matches macOS Chinese conventions.

10. **"创建设置"** (line 384) — GroupBox label. "创建设置" = "Creation settings". This is clear and concise.

11. **"用途"** (line 386), **"格式"** (line 387), **"压缩方式"** (line 388), **"加密"** (line 389) — Setting labels. All standard and clear.

12. **"保存位置"** (line 427) — GroupBox label. Standard and clear.

13. **"尚未选择"** (line 432) — Placeholder. "Not yet selected". Standard macOS Chinese convention. Fine.

14. **"选择…"** (line 437) — Button text with ellipsis. Standard macOS convention. Fine.

15. **"取消"** (line 463, 473) — Cancel. Standard. Fine.

16. **"创建归档"** (lines 350, 475) — Create archive action. Matches design spec section 7. Fine.

Now the error messages from AppModel.swift:

17. **"没有可归档的文件。"** (line 552) — "No archivable files." Clear but the triple-negative feeling is a bit stark. It's used when `noInputs` — the user provided no inputs. This is fine as an error message.

18. **"“\(name)”无法在 Windows 中安全使用，请重命名后再试。"** (line 554) — "X cannot be safely used on Windows, please rename and try again." The phrase "无法在 Windows 中安全使用" is a hard translation. In Chinese, a filename isn't "used" — it's "a valid filename" or "can be used as a filename". 

Better: `"\(name)" 在 Windows 中不是有效的文件名，请重命名后再试。`
Or: `"\(name)" 包含 Windows 不支持的字符，请重命名后再试。`

The word "安全" (safe/safely) is also a direct translation of "safe" which might not be the right concept — it's about compatibility/validity, not safety. Though "safe" is used in the design spec context to mean "compliant with Windows rules," in Chinese "安全使用" sounds like security risk.

Better: `"\(name)" 不符合 Windows 文件名规则，请重命名后再试。`

19. **"“\(first)”与“\(second)”在 Windows 中会发生名称冲突。"** (line 556) — "X and Y would have a name collision on Windows." Clear and accurate. The word "冲突" (collision/conflict) is appropriate. Fine.

20. **"“\(name)”不是可安全归档的普通文件或文件夹。"** (line 558) — "X is not a regular file or folder that can be safely archived." Again, "可安全归档" (safely archivable) is a hard translation. In Chinese, a file isn't "safely archivable" — it either is a regular file/folder (suitable for archiving) or isn't (e.g., symlink, socket, device file).

Better: `"\(name)" 不是普通文件或文件夹，无法归档。`
Or: `"\(name)" 是特殊文件类型，无法归档。`

The "安全" (safe/safely) here is also an over-translation.

21. **"目标位置已存在同名压缩包，未覆盖原文件。"** (line 560) — "A zip file with the same name already exists at the target location; original file was not overwritten." This is clear and well-structured. Good.

22. **"无法写入目标位置，未生成压缩包。"** (line 562) — "Cannot write to target location; no archive was generated." Clear. Good.

23. **"创建后的校验未通过，未发布压缩包。"** (line 566) — "Post-creation verification failed; archive not published." The word "发布" (publish) is an overclaim — this is a local file creation, not publication. Better: `创建后的校验未通过，未生成压缩包。` or `创建后的校验未通过，未保存压缩包。`

24. **"无法创建压缩包，未生成任何文件。"** (line 568) — "Cannot create archive; no files generated." General fallback. Good.

25. **"正在准备创建归档…"** (line 264) — Progress message. Fine.

26. **"正在创建 · \(...)/\(...) 项"** (line 281) — Progress message. Fine.

27. **"创建完成：\(outputURL.lastPathComponent)"** (line 298) — Completion message. Good.

28. **"创建完成"** (line 299) — Short status. Good.

29. **"已取消创建归档"** (line 304) — Cancelled message. Good.

30. **"创建失败"** (line 311) — Failure message. Good.

31. **"无法创建压缩包"** (line 140) — Alert title. Matches the general error pattern. Good.

Per design spec section 13.1, this claim is made with clear conditions (unencrypted ZIP, Store/Deflate, UTF-8, bit 11, no macOS metadata, pre-check passed). The claim is conditional on successful pre-check. Fine.

The claim doesn't cover Windows 10 or earlier. However, the tooltip/help text on the welcome button (line 98) says "Windows 11" which could be seen as overclaiming if older Windows versions can also open it. Now let me consolidate the must-change items:

**Must-change:**

1. Line 469: `"创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件。"` — Timing confusion. "创建完成前" (before creation completes) doesn't match the actual behavior (after creation). This misleads users about when verification happens.

2. Line 554: `"“\(name)”无法在 Windows 中安全使用，请重命名后再试。"` — "安全使用" is a hard translation. A filename isn't "used safely"; it either conforms to rules or doesn't.

Let me reconsider: Is #5 truly "must" or "optional"? The current string is understandable but slightly imprecise. I'll mark it as "must" because it's user-facing and changes the semantic center — the benefit is "Windows can open it directly," not "you can send it directly."

Actually, let me re-read: "创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP 压缩包". A reader could interpret this as: "Create ZIP archives that are in a format suitable for sending to Windows users (e.g., compatible format)." The emphasis on "发送" rather than "打开" shifts the meaning. The design spec is clear: the value proposition is Windows 11 can open it directly. So this should be: `创建 Windows 用户可直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包`.

6. Line 98: `"创建可由 Windows 11 直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包"` — Similar issue but slightly different. "可由" (can be) is passive, making "创建可由...打开的..." a bit clunky. Better: `创建 Windows 11 可直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包`.

**Consistency / Overclaim Audit:**

1. The design spec uses "Windows 11" specifically. The strings use "Windows 11" and "Windows" interchangeably:
   - Line 98: "Windows 11"
   - Line 233: "Windows" (generic)
   - Line 352: "Windows" (generic, in "Windows 用户")

Is this inconsistent? The design spec (section 19) specifies physical verification on "Windows 11 24H2 Explorer". But the error messages and compatibility checks apply to Windows in general (reserved names, illegal characters are consistent across Windows versions). The compatibility claim specifically says "Windows 11 可直接打开" because that's the verified target. Using "Windows" in error/check contexts is actually more accurate because the filename rules apply to Windows broadly, not just Windows 11. The specific claim "Windows 11 可直接打开" is appropriately scoped.

So this isn't truly inconsistent — it's correct:
- Compatibility CLAIM: "Windows 11" (verified target)
- Error/check context: "Windows" (rules apply broadly)

2. The tooltip on line 98 says "Windows 11" while the subtitle on line 352 says "Windows 用户". The tooltip is on the welcome screen button, the subtitle is in the creation panel. Both could use "Windows 11" for consistency, but "Windows 用户" (Windows users) is more natural in the context of "send to Windows users". I'll flag this as optional polish.

3. "归档" vs "压缩包" consistency: The design spec (section 7) says 压缩包 for user-facing, 归档 for system/command context. Let me check:
   - Line 97: `"创建归档"` — This is a user-facing button. But "创建归档" is the design spec's approved term for "create archive". ✅
   - Line 140: `"无法创建压缩包"` — User-facing alert. Uses 压缩包. Consistent with design spec? The spec says 压缩包 for user-facing. Alert is user-facing, uses 压缩包. But "创建归档" (button) vs "创建压缩包" (alert) — is this inconsistency?

Actually, the design spec section 7 says:
- archive noun: `压缩包` (user-facing) / `归档` (system/format context)
- create archive: `创建归档`

So the button says "创建归档" (the action), and the error says "无法创建压缩包" (the thing that couldn't be created). This is actually consistent with the design spec: the action is "创建归档", but the thing being created (the resulting file) is described as "压缩包" when mentioned in isolation.

Now wait — the footer note on line 469 says "创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件" — this uses neither 归档 nor 压缩包. But that's fine because it's referring to "全部文件" (all files), not to the archive itself.

The tooltip is associated with the button, not the final output, so it describes the intended/default behavior. This is arguably acceptable for a tooltip — it describes what the feature aims to do. Wait, I need to reconsider #5. Let me look at the string again: `"创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP 压缩包"`.

Breaking this down: "创建" (create) + "可直接发给 Windows 用户的" (that can be directly sent to Windows users) + "ZIP 压缩包" (ZIP archive).

The issue: "可直接" modifies "发给" (send). But the real value prop is "Windows users can directly open it" — the sending part is incidental; the user might put it on a USB drive, share via LAN, etc. The product's value is the compatibility, not the sending method.

Actually, re-reading the design spec section 5.3: "用途预设：发给 Windows 用户". The purpose/preset is "发给 Windows 用户" (for sending to Windows users). So the subtitle is actually describing the purpose of this preset. In that context, "创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP 压缩包" means "Create ZIP archives [of the type] that can be directly sent to Windows users [without compatibility worries]". The "直接" here means "without needing to explain how to open it / without needing special software on their end."

Hmm, but this interpretation makes the "直接" ambiguous — is it about sending directly (no intermediate conversion) or opening directly (no extra software)? Both readings are plausible. A native Chinese reader would likely interpret it as "no intermediate conversion needed before sending" which shifts focus from the actual benefit (Windows compatibility).

Let me settle on: this is ambiguous enough to be a must-change. The subtitle should clearly state the benefit: Windows users can open it directly.

Actually wait, I just want to double check — what if the intention really is "send directly"? Like "you can directly send this to Windows users because it's compatible"? In that case, "直接" is fine where it is, meaning "you don't need to do anything special before sending." But a better construction would be: `创建的 ZIP 压缩包，Windows 用户可直接打开` or `创建 Windows 用户可直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包`.

OK I'll keep it as must-change but with a clear rationale.

Now, for the must-change table, I think there's one more subtle issue I should check:

The string on line 233: `"可以选择多个文件和文件夹；创建前会检查 Windows 文件名兼容性。"` — This says "Windows 文件名兼容性" (Windows filename compatibility). But the actual check (as described on line 414) also covers: reserved names, illegal characters, name collisions, and macOS metadata exclusion. "文件名兼容性" might be narrow. But for a panel message (which is brief by design), it's acceptable as a summary. The detailed description is in the compatibility section of the panel. So this is fine.

Also checking the string on line 246: `"选择保存位置和文件名；已有文件不会被静默覆盖。"` — "静默覆盖" (silently overwrite) — this is the file-system-level overwrite behavior of the save panel. But wait, this is an NSSavePanel message. NSSavePanel typically shows a system-native "Replace" dialog if the file exists. This message might be describing custom behavior added by the app. Looking at the error code on line 559-560: `case .outputExists: return "目标位置已存在同名压缩包，未覆盖原文件。"` — So the app does check for existing files. The save panel message is accurate about the behavior. Fine.

One more thing — checking the consistency of Chinese punctuation:
- Line 233: Uses `；` (Chinese semicolon) — correct for Chinese text
- Line 246: Uses `；` (Chinese semicolon) — correct
- Line 414: Uses `；` (Chinese semicolon) for two separators — correct

These are all using proper Chinese punctuation, not Western semicolons. Good.

Now let me write the final output. I'll structure it exactly as the task requires.

Let me also make sure my analysis is grounded in evidence from the source files. Every claim should reference specific line numbers and file paths.

I notice I should also consider whether the string "创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件。" (line 469) could be interpreted differently. "创建完成前" in Chinese could mean "before declaring creation as complete" rather than "before the creation process finishes." In that reading, the message means: "Before we mark creation as complete, we'll reopen and verify all files." This is a process description — it tells the user what the final step of the process is. If read this way, it's actually accurate: the verification step happens as part of the creation process, before completion is declared.

But this is a subtle reading. The natural reading is "before creation finishes" (i.e., during the creation process), which implies the verification happens in parallel with or as part of the creation, which is misleading if the verification happens after the archive is written but before we tell the user it's done.

Actually, looking at the code flow:
1. `createWindowsZIP` creates the ZIP file
2. After creation succeeds (line 295): `apply(snapshot)` — this opens and applies the snapshot
3. Then `creationProgress = 1`, `lastCreatedURL = outputURL`, status/operation messages set

So the flow is: write ZIP → open and verify → mark complete. The "创建完成前" could mean "before we tell you it's complete, we verify." If the footer note is telling users what will happen after they click the button, this timing is actually accurate! The verification is the last step before completion is reported.

However, the phrasing is still ambiguous. "创建完成前" in common Chinese usage means "before [the act of] creation finishes" not "before [we report] creation as complete." And from a user perspective, they see a progress bar fill up during creation, and the verification might happen after the progress reaches 100% but before the success message — the user would see this as "after creation."

I'll keep this as must-change. A clearer phrase would be: `创建完成后将重新打开并核对全部文件。`

OK I'm confident now. Let me write the output.

+# Hermes Chinese Label Review: windows_zip_creation_chinese_labels
+
+Audit date: 2026-07-13
+Sources: `RootWindowView.swift`, `AppModel.swift`, `design/2026-07-11_full_design_spec.md` §7/§5.3/§13
+Scope: buttons, panel labels, explanatory text, compatibility statement, progress/cancel/completion text, and creation-specific error messages added for the Windows ZIP slice. Excludes pre-existing labels (open, extract, etc.).

+
+## Boundary Check
+
+- Workspace: `/Users/smkzw/Documents/AI Products/.worktrees/foundation` — all reads within this root.
+- Files read: exactly the four listed in the task prompt. No production paths, no unlisted files.
+- No edits to source files; no web, browser, test, or visual operations performed.

+## Must-Change Table
+
+| # | File:Line | Current | Proposed | Severity | Rationale |
+|---|---|---|---|---|---|
+| 1 | `RootWindowView.swift:469` | `创建完成前会重新打开并核对全部文件。` | `创建完成后将重新打开并核对全部文件。` | **must** | **动作/结果时序混淆**。"创建完成前"在中文中自然解读为"在创建动作完成之前"，暗示校验与创建并行或提前发生；但实际流程是：写入ZIP → 重新打开核对 → 报告完成。从用户视角看，进度条走完、文件已写出，此时"重新打开"发生在创建之后，而非之前。改用"创建完成后"可以准确表达"全部写完后还会再检查一遍"的保障语义；与设计规格§13.4（`创建成功不只看进程退出码：重新打开、检测完整性、核对条目清单`）一致。 |
+| 2 | `AppModel.swift:554` | `"\(name)"无法在 Windows 中安全使用，请重命名后再试。` | `"\(name)" 不符合 Windows 文件名规则，请重命名后再试。` | **must** | **硬翻译 / "安全"词义漂移**。"安全使用"对应英文 "safely used"，但文件名在中文里不是被"使用"的对象，而是"有效/合规"的问题。"安全"在此上下文中容易让用户误读为安全风险（恶意文件），实际含义是兼容性/合法性。设计规格§13.1 定义的是"Windows 保留名、非法字符"检查，改用"不符合...文件名规则"直接对应检查实质。 |
+| 3 | `AppModel.swift:558` | `"\(name)"不是可安全归档的普通文件或文件夹。` | `"\(name)" 是特殊文件类型，无法归档。` | **must** | **硬翻译 / "可安全归档"不自然**。"可安全归档"直译 "safely archivable"，中文中文件不是"安全归档"的——它是普通文件/文件夹就可以归档，是符号链接/设备文件/套接字就不能归档。当前措辞不仅生硬，还模糊了拒绝原因（不是普通文件）。改用"特殊文件类型，无法归档"直截了当，与错误码 `unsupportedItem` 语义对齐。 |
+| 4 | `AppModel.swift:566` | `创建后的校验未通过，未发布压缩包。` | `创建后的校验未通过，未生成压缩包。` | **must** | **动作夸大 / "发布"用词不当**。"发布"(publish)在软件语境中指向分发、上线、推送，这里是本地文件创建，校验失败后并没有走到"发布"阶段。同一文件中其他错误消息统一使用"未生成"（如 line 562 `未生成压缩包`、line 568 `未生成任何文件`），此处的"发布"破坏了消息体系的一致性。改用"未保存压缩包"或"未生成压缩包"均可；推荐"未生成"以保持与同类消息一致。 |
+| 5 | `RootWindowView.swift:352` | `创建可直接发给 Windows 用户的 ZIP 压缩包` | `创建 Windows 用户可直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包` | **must** | **语义重心偏移 / "直接"修饰错误动词**。当前"直接"修饰"发给"（发送），核心价值主张变成了"不需要中间转换就可以发送"，而产品真正的差异化价值是"Windows 端无需安装额外软件即可打开"——"直接"应修饰"打开"而非"发送"。设计规格§7 术语基线明确将核心兼容性声明定为 `Windows 11 可直接打开`，此处副标题应与之对齐。 |

+| 1 | `RootWindowView.swift:98` | `创建可由 Windows 11 直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包` | `创建 Windows 11 可直接打开的 ZIP 压缩包` | optional | **被动句式略冗余**。"可由...打开的"被动结构在中文中不算错误，但比主动句式长且弱。Help tooltip 应精炼直接；设计规格§7 和 line 44 `compatibility` 字段均使用主动句式 `Windows 11 可直接打开`，tooltip 与之统一更好。当前版本可接受，不改不阻塞。 |
